Woe names the outcome, while the fire sense of the prayer root supplies a concrete image of ordeal.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Forbearance After a Stumble","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1afe3fb5e5c60577c689","text":"107:2 يَدُعُّ (د ع ع), 107:4 مُصَلِّينَ and 107:5 صَلَاتِهِمْ (ص ل و), 107:5 سَاهُونَ (س ه و)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Trapping and Capture","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1ef4ea4837f2d053a702","text":"An actor controls access or escape by shoving, restraining, trapping, fortifying, or refusing entry.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B009","root_000879/B005","root_000934/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Anatomy and Bodily Condition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_23a1f158f1673635b290","text":"loins and tail flanks (ص ل و:B005/m01); lung (ر ء ي:B009/m01); pulmonary ailment (ر ء ي:B009/m02); livestock fat and marrow condition (ط ع م:B007/m01)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Bitter Medicinal Material","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_243b28d126ffafca4c14","text":"Water and human handling transform wild or cultivated plant material into pasture, food, or remedy.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Obedience, Prayer, and Blessing","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_25433c82d155f74b559a","text":"Religious obligation becomes visible through prayer, mercy, and social response, while denial, neglect, and display place the actor before reckoning and consequence.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000477/B003","root_000477/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Herding Call, Restorative Cry, and Lament","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2699e4bc1f22cf6a0178","text":"herding call and rebuke (د ع ع:B003/m01); restorative rise-call (د ع ع:B004/m01); blessing to be raised (د ع ع:B004/m02); lament cry (و ي ل:B002/m01); disgrace and calamity (و ي ل:B002/m02)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Hearth Warmth and Roasting","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4263451e172525cbdafd","text":"Fire creates a domestic center by warming bodies and transforming food. Its heat is welcomed and controlled, and the resulting comfort helps define the inhabited setting.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B006","root_000726/B009","root_000879/B007","root_001448/B003","root_004706/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Protected Civic and Sacred Center","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_426f62f0c5522bdcce5c","text":"city as a seat of obedience (د ي ن:B006/m01); house of worship (ص ل و:B007/m01); fortified place (م ن ع:B003/m02); defensive strength (م ن ع:B003/m01); communal religious due (م ع ن:B005/m03); settled place (س ك ن:B009/m01)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Horse Race and Second Position","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_42e66843919be8221f6c","text":"Movement is prompted, extended, slowed, interrupted, ranked, or stabilized by a controlling mechanism.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Obedience, Prayer, and Blessing","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_45023cd3b863335148ac","text":"A worshipper enters an order of obedience, performs prescribed prayer, and directs blessing or mercy toward another.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Negligent and Ostentatious Observance","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_481a86b68f5626d5e4d1","text":"Ritual action remains publicly visible while attention leaves the act and concern shifts toward the gaze of other people.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Obedience, Prayer, and Blessing","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4a9fa684c68d889e9d9a","text":"Obedience supplies the governing relation, ritual prayer supplies the enacted form, and blessing or mercy supplies the outward-directed effect. Prayer can also become a place of inward repose, while the religious-due sense of small aid carries observance into material service.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Protected Civic and Sacred Center","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4fa562a6637a945f05f9","text":"A town organizes obedience around a house of worship, maintains communal dues, and protects its inhabited territory.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Herding Call, Restorative Cry, and Lament","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_54b2dbc996a090c4c95b","text":"Directed utterances organize attention and action by asking, pointing, exhorting, prohibiting, calling, or lamenting.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002","root_000879/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Punitive Heat and Woe","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_54d3ed3467dc3f3a46a7","text":"exposure to heat and hardship (ص ل و:B001/m03); doom and destruction (و ي ل:B001/m01); lament and calamity (و ي ل:B002/m01); judgment and recompense (د ي ن:B002/m01)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Bitter Medicinal Material","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_586cef51e6b2687909b7","text":"The medicinal object is defined by bitterness, resinous matter, and an unusual animal-derived ingredient.
